+/*
+ * Generic SPI display panel for M5Stack ESP32-S3 boards.
+ *
+ * The StopWatch uses a QSPI CO5300 panel.  StickS3/AtomS3R/CoreS3 use
+ * ordinary SPI panels (ST7789/GC9107/ILI9342) with the command/data
+ * distinction carried by the GP-SPI command and MOSI phases.
+ *
+ * This model implements the common MIPI-DCS command subset used by
+ * M5GFX: CASET, RASET, RAMWR, MADCTL, COLMOD, sleep/display on/off and
+ * inversion.  It keeps a controller-sized RGB565 framebuffer and exposes
+ * the visible window (panel width/height with controller offsets) through
+ * a QEMU console.
+ *
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
+ */
+#include "qemu/osdep.h"
+#include "qemu/main-loop.h"
+#include "qapi/error.h"
+#include "qemu/timer.h"
+#include "hw/qdev-core.h"
+#include "hw/qdev-properties.h"
+#include "ui/console.h"
+#include "ui/input.h"
+#include "m5stopwatch.h"
+
+static unsigned m5_panel_trace_count;
+
+static void m5_panel_update(void *opaque);
+
+struct M5SpiPanelState {
+    DeviceState parent_obj;
+    QemuConsole *con;
+    QEMUTimer *refresh_timer;
+    QEMUBH *refresh_bh;
+    uint16_t *mem;
+    uint32_t mem_w, mem_h;
+    uint32_t vis_w, vis_h;
+    uint32_t off_x, off_y;
+    bool physical_invert;
+    bool controller_invert;
+    bool dirty;
+    bool disp_on;
+    bool sleep;
+    uint8_t madctl;
+    uint8_t colmod;
+    uint8_t cmd;
+    uint8_t params[4];
+    unsigned pidx;
+    uint16_t xs, xe, ys, ye, cx, cy;
+    bool pixel_mode;
+    uint8_t pix_hi;
+    bool pix_half;
+    uint32_t key_a_pin, key_b_pin;
+    uint32_t read_id;
+    uint32_t glass_rotation;
+    bool apply_madctl;
+    bool touch;
+    uint32_t touch_int_pin;
+    bool immediate_update;
+    bool keyboard;
+    bool plc_keys;
+    int mouse_x, mouse_y;
+    bool mouse_btn;
+    char *dump_path;
+};
+
+static void m5_panel_reset_window(M5SpiPanelState *s)
+{
+    s->cx = s->xs;
+    s->cy = s->ys;
+}
+
+static void m5_panel_put_pixel(M5SpiPanelState *s, uint16_t px)
+{
+    static unsigned pixel_trace_count;
+    int x = s->cx, y = s->cy;
+    if (s->apply_madctl) {
+        if (s->madctl & 0x20) { int t = x; x = y; y = t; }
+        if (s->madctl & 0x40) { x = s->mem_w - 1 - x; }
+        if (s->madctl & 0x80) { y = s->mem_h - 1 - y; }
+    }
+    if (getenv("M5SW_PANEL_PIXEL_TRACE") &&
+        (px == 0xf800 || px == 0x001f || px == 0x07e0)
+        && pixel_trace_count++ < 500) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[m5panel-pixel] (%d,%d)=%04x window=(%u,%u)-(%u,%u)\n",
+                x, y, px, s->xs, s->ys, s->xe, s->ye);
+    }
+    if (x >= 0 && x < (int)s->mem_w && y >= 0 && y < (int)s->mem_h) {
+        s->mem[y * s->mem_w + x] = px;
+    }
+    if (++s->cx > s->xe) {
+        s->cx = s->xs;
+        if (++s->cy > s->ye) {
+            s->cy = s->ys;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void m5_spi_panel_write(M5SpiPanelState *s, const uint8_t *buf, uint32_t len,
+                        bool command_phase)
+{
+    static unsigned stream_trace_count;
+    static unsigned stream_trace_group;
+    static unsigned dc_trace_count;
+    if (getenv("M5SW_PANEL_DC_TRACE") && len && dc_trace_count++ < 1000) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[m5panel-dc] phase=%d len=%u first=%02x\n",
+                command_phase, len, buf[0]);
+    }
+    if (getenv("M5SW_SPI_TRACE") && m5_panel_trace_count < 10000) {
+        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < len && m5_panel_trace_count < 10000; i++) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "[m5panel] %c %02x\n",
+                    command_phase ? 'C' : 'D', buf[i]);
+            m5_panel_trace_count++;
+        }
+    }
+    if (!command_phase && s->cmd == 0x2c && getenv("M5SW_PANEL_STREAM_TRACE")
+        && stream_trace_count < 6000) {
+        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < len && stream_trace_count < 6000; i++) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "[m5panel-stream] %02x\n", buf[i]);
+            stream_trace_count++;
+        }
+    }
+    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < len; i++) {
+        uint8_t b = buf[i];
+        if (command_phase) {
+            if (b == 0x2c) {
+                stream_trace_count = 0;
+                if (getenv("M5SW_PANEL_STREAM_TRACE")) {
+                    fprintf(stderr, "[m5panel-stream-group] %u\n",
+                            stream_trace_group++);
+                }
+            }
+            if (getenv("M5SW_SPI_WINDOW_TRACE") && (b == 0x2A || b == 0x2B)) {
+                fprintf(stderr, "[m5panel-window] cmd=%02x\n", b);
+            }
+            s->cmd = b;
+            s->pidx = 0;
+            s->pixel_mode = false;
+            switch (b) {
+            case 0x28: s->disp_on = false; s->dirty = true; break;
+            case 0x29: s->disp_on = true; s->dirty = true; break;
+            case 0x10: s->sleep = true; s->dirty = true; break;
+            case 0x11: s->sleep = false; s->dirty = true; break;
+            case 0x20: s->controller_invert = false; s->dirty = true; break;
+            case 0x21: s->controller_invert = true; s->dirty = true; break;
+            case 0x01:
+                s->disp_on = false;
+                s->sleep = true;
+                s->madctl = 0;
+                s->controller_invert = false;
+                break;
+            default:
+                break;
+            }
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        switch (s->cmd) {
+        case 0x2A:
+            if (getenv("M5SW_SPI_WINDOW_TRACE")) {
+                fprintf(stderr, "[m5panel-window] 2a[%u]=%02x\n", s->pidx, b);
+            }
+            if (s->pidx < 4) {
+                s->params[s->pidx++] = b;
+            }
+            if (s->pidx == 4) {
+                s->xs = (s->params[0] << 8) | s->params[1];
+                s->xe = (s->params[2] << 8) | s->params[3];
+            }
+            break;
+        case 0x2B:
+            if (getenv("M5SW_SPI_WINDOW_TRACE")) {
+                fprintf(stderr, "[m5panel-window] 2b[%u]=%02x\n", s->pidx, b);
+            }
+            if (s->pidx < 4) {
+                s->params[s->pidx++] = b;
+            }
+            if (s->pidx == 4) {
+                s->ys = (s->params[0] << 8) | s->params[1];
+                s->ye = (s->params[2] << 8) | s->params[3];
+            }
+            break;
+        case 0x2C:
+        case 0x3C:
+            if (!s->pixel_mode) {
+                m5_panel_reset_window(s);
+                s->pixel_mode = true;
+                s->pix_half = false;
+            }
+            if (!s->pix_half) {
+                s->pix_hi = b;
+                s->pix_half = true;
+            } else {
+                m5_panel_put_pixel(s, (s->pix_hi << 8) | b);
+                s->pix_half = false;
+                s->dirty = true;
+            }
+            break;
+        case 0x36:
+            s->madctl = b;
+            s->dirty = true;
+            break;
+        case 0x3A:
+            s->colmod = b;
+            break;
+        default:
+            break;
+        }
+    }
+    if (s->immediate_update && s->dirty) {
+        qemu_bh_schedule(s->refresh_bh);
+        qemu_clock_notify(QEMU_CLOCK_VIRTUAL);
+    }
+}
+
+uint32_t m5_spi_panel_read_id(M5SpiPanelState *s)
+{
+    return s->read_id;
+}
+
+static void m5_panel_update(void *opaque)
+{
+    M5SpiPanelState *s = opaque;
+    DisplaySurface *surf = qemu_console_surface(s->con);
+    if (!surf || surface_bits_per_pixel(surf) != 32 ||
+        surface_width(surf) != s->vis_w || surface_height(surf) != s->vis_h) {
+        surf = qemu_create_displaysurface(s->vis_w, s->vis_h);
+        dpy_gfx_replace_surface(s->con, surf);
+        s->dirty = true;
+    }
+    if (!s->dirty) {
+        return;
+    }
+    s->dirty = false;
+    uint32_t *dst = (uint32_t *)surface_data(surf);
+    int stride = surface_stride(surf) / 4;
+    bool on = s->disp_on && !s->sleep;
+    bool invert = s->physical_invert ^ s->controller_invert;
+    for (uint32_t y = 0; y < s->vis_h; y++) {
+        uint32_t *d = dst + y * stride;
+        for (uint32_t x = 0; x < s->vis_w; x++) {
+            uint32_t mx = x + s->off_x;
+            uint32_t my = y + s->off_y;
+            switch (s->glass_rotation & 3) {
+            case 1:
+                mx = s->off_x + y;
+                my = s->off_y + s->vis_w - 1 - x;
+                break;
+            case 2:
+                mx = s->off_x + s->vis_w - 1 - x;
+                my = s->off_y + s->vis_h - 1 - y;
+                break;
+            case 3:
+                mx = s->off_x + s->vis_h - 1 - y;
+                my = s->off_y + x;
+                break;
+            default:
+                break;
+            }
+            uint16_t p = s->mem[my * s->mem_w + mx];
+            if (invert) {
+                p = ~p;
+            }
+            uint32_t r = ((p >> 11) & 0x1f) * 255 / 31;
+            uint32_t g = ((p >> 5) & 0x3f) * 255 / 63;
+            uint32_t b = (p & 0x1f) * 255 / 31;
+            if (!on) {
+                r = g = b = 0;
+            }
+            if (getenv("M5SW_PANEL_REFRESH_TRACE") && x == 20 && y == 20) {
+                fprintf(stderr,
+                        "[m5panel-pixel-render] mx=%u my=%u p=%04x on=%d inv=%d\n",
+                        mx, my, p, on, invert);
+            }
+            d[x] = 0xff000000u | (r << 16) | (g << 8) | b;
+        }
+    }
+    if (getenv("M5SW_PANEL_REFRESH_TRACE")) {
+        int stride_dbg = surface_stride(surf) / 4;
+        uint32_t *dbg = (uint32_t *)surface_data(surf);
+        fprintf(stderr, "[m5panel-render] surface=%p sample=%08x size=%ux%u\n",
+                (void *)surf, dbg[20 * stride_dbg + 20],
+                surface_width(surf), surface_height(surf));
+    }
+    dpy_gfx_update(s->con, 0, 0, s->vis_w, s->vis_h);
+}
+
+static void m5_panel_invalidate(void *opaque)
+{
+    ((M5SpiPanelState *)opaque)->dirty = true;
+}
+
+static const GraphicHwOps m5_panel_ops = {
+    .invalidate = m5_panel_invalidate,
+    .gfx_update = m5_panel_update,
+};
+
+static uint32_t m5_panel_pixel_rgb(M5SpiPanelState *s, uint32_t x, uint32_t y)
+{
+    uint32_t mx = x + s->off_x;
+    uint32_t my = y + s->off_y;
+
+    switch (s->glass_rotation & 3) {
+    case 1:
+        mx = s->off_x + y;
+        my = s->off_y + s->vis_w - 1 - x;
+        break;
+    case 2:
+        mx = s->off_x + s->vis_w - 1 - x;
+        my = s->off_y + s->vis_h - 1 - y;
+        break;
+    case 3:
+        mx = s->off_x + s->vis_h - 1 - y;
+        my = s->off_y + x;
+        break;
+    default:
+        break;
+    }
+
+    uint16_t p = s->mem[my * s->mem_w + mx];
+    bool on = s->disp_on && !s->sleep;
+    bool invert = s->physical_invert ^ s->controller_invert;
+    if (invert) {
+        p = ~p;
+    }
+    uint32_t r = ((p >> 11) & 0x1f) * 255 / 31;
+    uint32_t g = ((p >> 5) & 0x3f) * 255 / 63;
+    uint32_t b = (p & 0x1f) * 255 / 31;
+    if (!on) {
+        r = g = b = 0;
+    }
+    return (r << 16) | (g << 8) | b;
+}
+
+static void m5_panel_dump_ppm(M5SpiPanelState *s)
+{
+    if (!s->dump_path) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    FILE *f = fopen(s->dump_path, "wb");
+    if (!f) {
+        return;
+    }
+    fprintf(f, "P6\n%u %u\n255\n", s->vis_w, s->vis_h);
+    for (uint32_t y = 0; y < s->vis_h; y++) {
+        for (uint32_t x = 0; x < s->vis_w; x++) {
+            uint32_t rgb = m5_panel_pixel_rgb(s, x, y);
+            uint8_t bytes[3] = { rgb >> 16, rgb >> 8, rgb };
+            fwrite(bytes, sizeof(bytes), 1, f);
+        }
+    }
+    fclose(f);
+}
+
+static void m5_panel_refresh_tick(void *opaque)
+{
+    M5SpiPanelState *s = opaque;
+    int64_t now = qemu_clock_get_ns(QEMU_CLOCK_VIRTUAL);
+    if (getenv("M5SW_PANEL_REFRESH_TRACE") && s->dirty) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[m5panel-refresh] dirty sample=%04x\n",
+                s->mem[20 * s->mem_w + 20]);
+    }
+    m5_panel_update(s);
+    timer_mod(s->refresh_timer, now + 16666667);
+}
+
+static void m5_panel_refresh_bh(void *opaque)
+{
+    m5_panel_update(opaque);
+}
+
+static char *m5_panel_get_dump_path(Object *obj, Error **errp)
+{
+    return g_strdup(((M5SpiPanelState *)obj)->dump_path);
+}
+
+static void m5_panel_set_dump_path(Object *obj, const char *value, Error **errp)
+{
+    M5SpiPanelState *s = (M5SpiPanelState *)obj;
+    g_free(s->dump_path);
+    s->dump_path = g_strdup(value);
+}
+
+static bool m5_panel_get_refresh_now(Object *obj, Error **errp)
+{
+    return false;
+}
+
+static void m5_panel_set_refresh_now(Object *obj, bool value, Error **errp)
+{
+    M5SpiPanelState *s = (M5SpiPanelState *)obj;
+    if (value) {
+        s->dirty = true;
+        m5_panel_update(s);
+        m5_panel_dump_ppm(s);
+    }
+}
+
+static void m5_panel_input(DeviceState *dev, QemuConsole *src, InputEvent *evt)
+{
+    M5SpiPanelState *s = M5_SPI_PANEL(dev);
+    if (getenv("M5SW_INPUT_TRACE")) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[m5panel-input] type=%d\n", evt->type);
+    }
+    switch (evt->type) {
+    case INPUT_EVENT_KIND_KEY: {
+        InputKeyEvent *key = evt->u.key.data;
+        int q = qemu_input_key_value_to_qcode(key->key);
+        int down = key->down;
+        if (getenv("M5SW_INPUT_TRACE")) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "[m5panel-input] qcode=%d down=%d\n", q, down);
+        }
+        if (s->keyboard) {
+            uint8_t event = down ? 0x81 : 0x01;
+            m5sw_keyboard_event(event);
+            break;
+        }
+        if (s->plc_keys) {
+            if (q == Q_KEY_CODE_A) {
+                m5sw_plc_key_set(0, down);
+            } else if (q == Q_KEY_CODE_B) {
+                m5sw_plc_key_set(1, down);
+            } else if (q == Q_KEY_CODE_C) {
+                m5sw_plc_key_set(2, down);
+            }
+            break;
+        }
+        switch (q) {
+        case Q_KEY_CODE_A: case Q_KEY_CODE_LEFT: case Q_KEY_CODE_1:
+            esp32_gpio_set_input(s->key_a_pin, !down);
+            break;
+        case Q_KEY_CODE_B: case Q_KEY_CODE_RIGHT: case Q_KEY_CODE_2:
+            esp32_gpio_set_input(s->key_b_pin, !down);
+            break;
+        case Q_KEY_CODE_P: case Q_KEY_CODE_3:
+            m5sw_pwr_button(down);
+            break;
+        default:
+            break;
+        }
+        break;
+    }
+    case INPUT_EVENT_KIND_ABS: {
+        InputMoveEvent *m = evt->u.abs.data;
+        int v = qemu_input_scale_axis(m->value, INPUT_EVENT_ABS_MIN,
+                                      INPUT_EVENT_ABS_MAX, 0, s->vis_w - 1);
+        if (m->axis == INPUT_AXIS_X) {
+            s->mouse_x = v;
+        } else if (m->axis == INPUT_AXIS_Y) {
+            s->mouse_y = qemu_input_scale_axis(m->value, INPUT_EVENT_ABS_MIN,
+                                               INPUT_EVENT_ABS_MAX, 0, s->vis_h - 1);
+        }
+        break;
+    }
+    case INPUT_EVENT_KIND_BTN: {
+        InputBtnEvent *b = evt->u.btn.data;
+        if (b->button == INPUT_BUTTON_LEFT) {
+            s->mouse_btn = b->down;
+        }
+        break;
+    }
+    default:
+        break;
+    }
+}
+
+static void m5_panel_input_sync(DeviceState *dev)
+{
+    M5SpiPanelState *s = M5_SPI_PANEL(dev);
+    if (s->touch) {
+        m5sw_ft_touch_set_pin(s->mouse_btn, s->mouse_x, s->mouse_y,
+                              s->touch_int_pin);
+    }
+}
+
+static const QemuInputHandler m5_panel_input_handler = {
+    .name = "M5Stack SPI panel keys",
+    .mask = INPUT_EVENT_MASK_KEY | INPUT_EVENT_MASK_BTN | INPUT_EVENT_MASK_ABS,
+    .event = m5_panel_input,
+    .sync = m5_panel_input_sync,
+};
+
+static void m5_panel_realize(DeviceState *dev, Error **errp)
+{
+    M5SpiPanelState *s = M5_SPI_PANEL(dev);
+    s->mem = g_new0(uint16_t, s->mem_w * s->mem_h);
+    s->con = graphic_console_init(dev, 0, &m5_panel_ops, s);
+    qemu_console_resize(s->con, s->vis_w, s->vis_h);
+    s->dirty = true;
+    s->sleep = true;
+    s->xe = s->mem_w - 1;
+    s->ye = s->mem_h - 1;
+    m5_panel_reset_window(s);
+    s->refresh_timer = timer_new_ns(QEMU_CLOCK_VIRTUAL,
+                                    m5_panel_refresh_tick, s);
+    s->refresh_bh = qemu_bh_new(m5_panel_refresh_bh, s);
+    timer_mod(s->refresh_timer,
+              qemu_clock_get_ns(QEMU_CLOCK_VIRTUAL) + 16666667);
+    QemuInputHandlerState *hs = qemu_input_handler_register(dev, &m5_panel_input_handler);
+    qemu_input_handler_activate(hs);
+    object_property_add_str(OBJECT(s), "dump-path",
+                            m5_panel_get_dump_path, m5_panel_set_dump_path);
+    object_property_add_bool(OBJECT(s), "refresh-now",
+                             m5_panel_get_refresh_now, m5_panel_set_refresh_now);
+}
+
+static Property m5_panel_props[] = {
+    DEFINE_PROP_UINT32("mem-width", M5SpiPanelState, mem_w, 240),
+    DEFINE_PROP_UINT32("mem-height", M5SpiPanelState, mem_h, 320),
+    DEFINE_PROP_UINT32("vis-width", M5SpiPanelState, vis_w, 135),
+    DEFINE_PROP_UINT32("vis-height", M5SpiPanelState, vis_h, 240),
+    DEFINE_PROP_UINT32("offset-x", M5SpiPanelState, off_x, 52),
+    DEFINE_PROP_UINT32("offset-y", M5SpiPanelState, off_y, 40),
+    DEFINE_PROP_UINT32("key-a-pin", M5SpiPanelState, key_a_pin, 10),
+    DEFINE_PROP_UINT32("key-b-pin", M5SpiPanelState, key_b_pin, 9),
+    DEFINE_PROP_UINT32("read-id", M5SpiPanelState, read_id, 0x00007683),
+    DEFINE_PROP_UINT32("glass-rotation", M5SpiPanelState, glass_rotation, 0),
+    DEFINE_PROP_BOOL("apply-madctl", M5SpiPanelState, apply_madctl, true),
+    DEFINE_PROP_BOOL("touch", M5SpiPanelState, touch, false),
+    DEFINE_PROP_UINT32("touch-int-pin", M5SpiPanelState, touch_int_pin, 21),
+    DEFINE_PROP_BOOL("immediate-update", M5SpiPanelState, immediate_update, false),
+    DEFINE_PROP_BOOL("keyboard", M5SpiPanelState, keyboard, false),
+    DEFINE_PROP_BOOL("plc-keys", M5SpiPanelState, plc_keys, false),
+    DEFINE_PROP_BOOL("invert", M5SpiPanelState, physical_invert, true),
+    DEFINE_PROP_END_OF_LIST(),
+};
+
+static void m5_panel_class_init(ObjectClass *klass, void *data)
+{
+    DeviceClass *dc = DEVICE_CLASS(klass);
+    dc->realize = m5_panel_realize;
+    device_class_set_props(dc, m5_panel_props);
+}
+
+static const TypeInfo m5_panel_info = {
+    .name = TYPE_M5_SPI_PANEL,
+    .parent = TYPE_DEVICE,
+    .instance_size = sizeof(M5SpiPanelState),
+    .class_init = m5_panel_class_init,
+};
+
+static void m5_panel_register(void)
+{
+    type_register_static(&m5_panel_info);
+}
+type_init(m5_panel_register)
diff --git a/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_st25r3916_spi.c b/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_st25r3916_spi.c
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..2645eb2
--- /dev/null
+++ b/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_st25r3916_spi.c
@@ -0,0 +1,258 @@
+/*
+ * ST25R3916 NFC reader SPI transport model.
+ *
+ * UIFlow2 uses command/register writes, register reads, Space-B accesses,
+ * FIFO load/read and ISO14443A command responses over this transport. This
+ * model keeps the same deterministic NTAG behavior as the I2C model while
+ * allowing ST25R3916 to share an SPI bus with SD and CC1101 chip selects.
+ *
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
+ */
+#include "qemu/osdep.h"
+#include "hw/gpio/esp32_gpio.h"
+#include "m5stopwatch.h"
+
+#define ST25_FIFO_SIZE 512
+#define ST25_TAG_SIZE  180
+
+enum {
+    ST25_IDLE,
+    ST25_REG_WRITE,
+    ST25_REG_READ,
+    ST25_SPACE_ADDR,
+    ST25_SPACE_WRITE,
+    ST25_SPACE_READ,
+    ST25_FIFO_WRITE,
+    ST25_FIFO_READ,
+};
+
+typedef struct M5St25r3916SpiState {
+    uint8_t regs[64];
+    uint8_t space_b[64];
+    uint8_t tx[64];
+    uint32_t tx_len;
+    uint8_t rx[ST25_FIFO_SIZE];
+    uint32_t rx_len;
+    uint32_t rx_pos;
+    uint8_t tag[ST25_TAG_SIZE];
+    uint8_t phase;
+    uint8_t reg;
+    uint8_t space_addr;
+    bool field_on;
+    int irq_pin;
+} M5St25r3916SpiState;
+
+static void st25_set_fifo(M5St25r3916SpiState *s,
+                          const uint8_t *data, uint32_t size)
+{
+    size = MIN(size, sizeof(s->rx));
+    if (size) {
+        memcpy(s->rx, data, size);
+    }
+    s->rx_len = size;
+    s->rx_pos = 0;
+    if (s->irq_pin >= 0) {
+        esp32_gpio_force_line(s->irq_pin, size != 0);
+    }
+}
+
+static void st25_set_fifo_byte(M5St25r3916SpiState *s, uint8_t value)
+{
+    st25_set_fifo(s, &value, 1);
+}
+
+static void st25_init_tag(M5St25r3916SpiState *s)
+{
+    static const uint8_t uid[4] = { 0xde, 0xad, 0xbe, 0xef };
+    static const uint8_t page4[4] = { 'M', '5', 'N', '1' };
+
+    memcpy(s->tag, uid, sizeof(uid));
+    s->tag[4] = uid[0] ^ uid[1] ^ uid[2] ^ uid[3];
+    s->tag[5] = 0x48;
+    s->tag[12] = 0xe1;
+    s->tag[13] = 0x10;
+    s->tag[14] = 0x12;
+    memcpy(&s->tag[16], page4, sizeof(page4));
+}
+
+static void st25_execute_command(M5St25r3916SpiState *s, uint8_t command)
+{
+    static const uint8_t atqa[2] = { 0x44, 0x00 };
+    static const uint8_t version[10] = {
+        0x00, 0x04, 0x04, 0x02, 0x02, 0x01, 0x0b, 0x03, 0x00, 0x00,
+    };
+    uint8_t response[18] = { 0 };
+
+    switch (command) {
+    case 0xc1: /* SET_DEFAULT */
+        memset(s->regs, 0, sizeof(s->regs));
+        memset(s->space_b, 0, sizeof(s->space_b));
+        s->regs[0x3f] = 0x2a;
+        st25_set_fifo(s, NULL, 0);
+        break;
+    case 0xc6: /* TRANSMIT_REQA */
+    case 0xc7: /* TRANSMIT_WUPA */
+        st25_set_fifo(s, atqa, sizeof(atqa));
+        break;
+    case 0xc5: /* TRANSMIT_WITHOUT_CRC */
+        if (s->tx_len >= 1 && s->tx[0] == 0x93) {
+            response[0] = s->tag[0];
+            response[1] = s->tag[1];
+            response[2] = s->tag[2];
+            response[3] = s->tag[3];
+            response[4] = s->tag[4];
+            st25_set_fifo(s, response, 5);
+        }
+        s->tx_len = 0;
+        break;
+    case 0xc4: /* TRANSMIT_WITH_CRC */
+        if (s->tx_len >= 2 && s->tx[0] == 0x93) {
+            response[0] = 0x00;
+            st25_set_fifo(s, response, 1);
+        } else if (s->tx_len >= 1 && s->tx[0] == 0x60) {
+            st25_set_fifo(s, version, sizeof(version));
+        } else if (s->tx_len >= 2 && s->tx[0] == 0x30) {
+            uint32_t base = (s->tx[1] & ~3u) * 4;
+            uint32_t available = sizeof(s->tag) - MIN(base, sizeof(s->tag));
+            uint32_t copy = MIN(available, 16);
+            memcpy(response, &s->tag[base], copy);
+            st25_set_fifo(s, response, copy + 2);
+        } else if (s->tx_len >= 6 && s->tx[0] == 0xa2) {
+            uint32_t offset = (s->tx[1] & 0x3f) * 4;
+            if (offset + 4 <= sizeof(s->tag)) {
+                memcpy(&s->tag[offset], &s->tx[2], 4);
+            }
+            st25_set_fifo_byte(s, 0x0a);
+        } else if (s->tx_len >= 1 && s->tx[0] == 0x50) {
+            st25_set_fifo(s, NULL, 0);
+        }
+        s->tx_len = 0;
+        break;
+    case 0xc8: /* NFC_INITIAL_FIELD_ON */
+        s->field_on = true;
+        s->regs[0x1a] |= 0x80;
+        break;
+    case 0xc2: /* STOP_ALL_ACTIVITIES */
+        s->field_on = false;
+        break;
+    case 0xdb: /* CLEAR_FIFO */
+        st25_set_fifo(s, NULL, 0);
+        s->tx_len = 0;
+        break;
+    default:
+        break;
+    }
+}
+
+static uint8_t st25_read_reg(M5St25r3916SpiState *s, uint8_t reg)
+{
+    uint32_t count;
+
+    switch (reg & 0x3f) {
+    case 0x1e:
+        count = MIN(s->rx_len, 0x3ff);
+        return count >> 8;
+    case 0x1f:
+        count = MIN(s->rx_len, 0x3ff);
+        return (count & 0x3f) << 6;
+    case 0x1a:
+        return s->regs[0x1a] |
+               ((s->field_on || (s->regs[0x02] & 0x80)) ? 0x80 : 0);
+    case 0x31:
+        return 0x10;
+    case 0x3f:
+        return 0x2a;
+    default:
+        return s->regs[reg & 0x3f];
+    }
+}
+
+static uint8_t st25_transfer(void *opaque, uint8_t tx)
+{
+    M5St25r3916SpiState *s = opaque;
+    uint8_t value = 0;
+
+    if (getenv("M5SW_SPI_XFER_TRACE")) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[st25-spi] phase=%u tx=%02x\n", s->phase, tx);
+    }
+    if (s->phase == ST25_IDLE) {
+        if (tx <= 0x3f) {
+            s->reg = tx & 0x3f;
+            s->phase = ST25_REG_WRITE;
+        } else if (tx <= 0x7f) {
+            s->reg = tx & 0x3f;
+            s->phase = ST25_REG_READ;
+        } else if (tx == 0xfb) {
+            s->phase = ST25_SPACE_ADDR;
+        } else if (tx == 0x80) {
+            s->tx_len = 0;
+            s->phase = ST25_FIFO_WRITE;
+        } else if (tx == 0x9f) {
+            s->rx_pos = 0;
+            s->phase = ST25_FIFO_READ;
+        } else {
+            st25_execute_command(s, tx);
+        }
+        return value;
+    }
+
+    switch (s->phase) {
+    case ST25_REG_WRITE:
+        s->regs[s->reg] = tx;
+        s->phase = ST25_IDLE;
+        break;
+    case ST25_REG_READ:
+        value = st25_read_reg(s, s->reg++);
+        break;
+    case ST25_SPACE_ADDR:
+        s->space_addr = tx & 0x3f;
+        s->phase = (tx & 0x40) ? ST25_SPACE_READ : ST25_SPACE_WRITE;
+        break;
+    case ST25_SPACE_WRITE:
+        s->space_b[s->space_addr] = tx;
+        s->phase = ST25_IDLE;
+        break;
+    case ST25_SPACE_READ:
+        value = s->space_b[s->space_addr++ & 0x3f];
+        break;
+    case ST25_FIFO_WRITE:
+        if (s->tx_len < sizeof(s->tx)) {
+            s->tx[s->tx_len++] = tx;
+        }
+        break;
+    case ST25_FIFO_READ:
+        value = s->rx_pos < s->rx_len ? s->rx[s->rx_pos++] : 0;
+        break;
+    default:
+        break;
+    }
+    return value;
+}
+
+static void st25_set_cs(void *opaque, int level)
+{
+    M5St25r3916SpiState *s = opaque;
+
+    if (level) {
+        s->phase = ST25_IDLE;
+        s->reg = 0;
+        s->space_addr = 0;
+    }
+}
+
+static const M5SpiSlaveOps st25_ops = {
+    .set_cs = st25_set_cs,
+    .transfer = st25_transfer,
+};
+
+void m5_st25r3916_spi_attach(DeviceState *spi, int cs_pin, int irq_pin)
+{
+    M5St25r3916SpiState *s = g_new0(M5St25r3916SpiState, 1);
+
+    s->irq_pin = irq_pin;
+    s->regs[0x3f] = 0x2a;
+    st25_init_tag(s);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_attach_spi_slave(spi, &st25_ops, s, cs_pin);
+    esp32_gpio_set_input(irq_pin, 0);
+    esp32_gpio_force_line(irq_pin, 0);
+}
diff --git a/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_sx1262.c b/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_sx1262.c
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..eafab65
--- /dev/null
+++ b/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_sx1262.c
@@ -0,0 +1,247 @@
+/*
+ * Minimal Semtech SX1262 SPI modem model for the UIFlow2 LoRa driver.
+ *
+ * This implements the command/status/register/FIFO protocol and a local TX
+ * loopback packet path. RF modulation and multi-device propagation are not
+ * modeled; packets sent by this modem become the next received packet.
+ *
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
+ */
+#include "qemu/osdep.h"
+#include "m5stopwatch.h"
+
+#define SX1262_MAX_PACKET 255
+
+typedef enum {
+    SX_IDLE,
+    SX_ARGS,
+    SX_RESPONSE,
+    SX_WRITE_BUFFER,
+    SX_READ_BUFFER,
+} Sx1262Mode;
+
+typedef struct M5Sx1262State {
+    const M5SpiSlaveOps *ops;
+    Sx1262Mode mode;
+    uint8_t opcode;
+    uint8_t args[16];
+    uint8_t arg_count;
+    uint8_t expected_args;
+    uint8_t response[264];
+    uint16_t response_len;
+    uint16_t response_pos;
+    uint8_t tx_packet[SX1262_MAX_PACKET];
+    uint8_t rx_packet[SX1262_MAX_PACKET];
+    uint16_t tx_len;
+    uint16_t rx_len;
+    uint16_t rx_pos;
+    uint16_t irq_status;
+    uint16_t registers[16];
+} M5Sx1262State;
+
+static uint8_t sx1262_status(void)
+{
+    return 0x20; /* STDBY_RC, command status OK */
+}
+
+static void sx1262_set_response(M5Sx1262State *s,
+                                const uint8_t *data, uint16_t size)
+{
+    memcpy(s->response, data, MIN(size, sizeof(s->response)));
+    s->response_len = MIN(size, sizeof(s->response));
+    s->response_pos = 0;
+    s->mode = SX_RESPONSE;
+}
+
+static void sx1262_command(M5Sx1262State *s)
+{
+    uint8_t response[4] = { sx1262_status(), 0, 0, 0 };
+
+    switch (s->opcode) {
+    case 0x02: /* CLR_IRQ_STATUS */
+        s->irq_status &= ~(((uint16_t)s->args[0] << 8) | s->args[1]);
+        s->mode = SX_IDLE;
+        break;
+    case 0x07: /* CLR_ERRORS */
+        s->mode = SX_IDLE;
+        break;
+    case 0x08: /* CFG_DIO_IRQ */
+        s->mode = SX_IDLE;
+        break;
+    case 0x0d: { /* WRITE_REGISTER */
+        uint16_t addr = ((uint16_t)s->args[0] << 8) | s->args[1];
+        s->registers[(addr >> 4) & 0xf] = s->args[2];
+        s->mode = SX_IDLE;
+        break;
+    }
+    case 0x0e: /* WRITE_BUFFER */
+        s->tx_len = 0;
+        s->mode = SX_WRITE_BUFFER;
+        break;
+    case 0x12: /* GET_IRQ_STATUS */
+        response[1] = s->irq_status >> 8;
+        response[2] = s->irq_status;
+        sx1262_set_response(s, response, 3);
+        break;
+    case 0x13: /* GET_RX_BUFFER_STATUS */
+        response[1] = s->rx_len;
+        response[2] = 0;
+        sx1262_set_response(s, response, 3);
+        break;
+    case 0x14: /* GET_PACKET_STATUS */
+        response[1] = 0xb0; /* RSSI */
+        response[2] = 0x08; /* SNR */
+        response[3] = 0xb0;
+        sx1262_set_response(s, response, 4);
+        break;
+    case 0x17: /* GET_ERROR */
+        sx1262_set_response(s, response, 3);
+        break;
+    case 0x1d: { /* READ_REGISTER */
+        uint16_t addr = ((uint16_t)s->args[0] << 8) | s->args[1];
+        response[0] = s->registers[(addr >> 4) & 0xf];
+        sx1262_set_response(s, response, 1);
+        break;
+    }
+    case 0x1e: /* READ_BUFFER */
+        s->rx_pos = 0;
+        response[0] = sx1262_status();
+        sx1262_set_response(s, response, 1);
+        s->mode = SX_READ_BUFFER;
+        break;
+    case 0x80: /* SET_STANDBY */
+    case 0x84: /* SET_SLEEP */
+    case 0x82: /* SET_RX */
+    case 0x83: /* SET_TX */
+    case 0x86: /* SET_RF_FREQUENCY */
+    case 0x89: /* CALIBRATE */
+    case 0x8a: /* SET_PACKET_TYPE */
+    case 0x8b: /* SET_MODULATION_PARAMS */
+    case 0x8c: /* SET_PACKET_PARAMS */
+    case 0x8e: /* SET_TX_PARAMS */
+    case 0x8f: /* SET_BUFFER_BASE_ADDRESS */
+    case 0x95: /* SET_PA_CONFIG */
+    case 0x97: /* SET_DIO3_AS_TCXO_CTRL */
+    case 0x98: /* CALIBRATE_IMAGE */
+    case 0x9d: /* SET_DIO2_AS_RF_SWITCH_CTRL */
+        if (s->opcode == 0x83) {
+            memcpy(s->rx_packet, s->tx_packet, s->tx_len);
+            s->rx_len = s->tx_len;
+            s->irq_status = 1; /* TX_DONE */
+        } else if (s->opcode == 0x82 && s->rx_len) {
+            s->irq_status = 2; /* RX_DONE */
+        }
+        s->mode = SX_IDLE;
+        break;
+    case 0xc0: /* GET_STATUS */
+        response[0] = sx1262_status();
+        sx1262_set_response(s, response, 1);
+        break;
+    default:
+        s->mode = SX_IDLE;
+        break;
+    }
+}
+
+static uint8_t sx1262_transfer(void *opaque, uint8_t tx)
+{
+    M5Sx1262State *s = opaque;
+    if (getenv("M5SW_SX1262_TRACE")) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[sx1262] mode=%d tx=%02x\n", s->mode, tx);
+    }
+
+    switch (s->mode) {
+    case SX_IDLE:
+        s->opcode = tx;
+        s->arg_count = 0;
+        s->expected_args = 0;
+        s->response_pos = 0;
+        s->response_len = 0;
+        switch (tx) {
+        case 0x02: s->expected_args = 2; break;
+        case 0x07: s->expected_args = 1; break;
+        case 0x08: s->expected_args = 8; break;
+        case 0x0d: s->expected_args = 3; break;
+        case 0x0e: s->expected_args = 1; break;
+        case 0x1d: s->expected_args = 3; break;
+        case 0x1e: s->expected_args = 1; break;
+        case 0x80: s->expected_args = 1; break;
+        case 0x82: s->expected_args = 3; break;
+        case 0x83: s->expected_args = 3; break;
+        case 0x84: s->expected_args = 1; break;
+        case 0x86: s->expected_args = 4; break;
+        case 0x89: s->expected_args = 1; break;
+        case 0x8a: s->expected_args = 1; break;
+        case 0x8b: s->expected_args = 4; break;
+        case 0x8c: s->expected_args = 6; break;
+        case 0x8e: s->expected_args = 2; break;
+        case 0x8f: s->expected_args = 2; break;
+        case 0x95: s->expected_args = 4; break;
+        case 0x97: s->expected_args = 4; break;
+        case 0x98: s->expected_args = 2; break;
+        case 0x9d: s->expected_args = 1; break;
+        default: break;
+        }
+        if (s->expected_args == 0) {
+            sx1262_command(s);
+        } else {
+            s->mode = SX_ARGS;
+        }
+        return sx1262_status();
+    case SX_ARGS:
+        if (s->arg_count < sizeof(s->args)) {
+            s->args[s->arg_count] = tx;
+        }
+        s->arg_count++;
+        if (s->arg_count >= s->expected_args) {
+            sx1262_command(s);
+        }
+        return sx1262_status();
+    case SX_RESPONSE:
+        if (s->response_pos < s->response_len) {
+            return s->response[s->response_pos++];
+        }
+        return sx1262_status();
+    case SX_WRITE_BUFFER:
+        if (s->tx_len < SX1262_MAX_PACKET) {
+            s->tx_packet[s->tx_len++] = tx;
+        }
+        return sx1262_status();
+    case SX_READ_BUFFER:
+        if (!s->response_pos) {
+            s->response_pos++;
+            return sx1262_status();
+        }
+        if (s->rx_pos < s->rx_len) {
+            return s->rx_packet[s->rx_pos++];
+        }
+        return 0;
+    default:
+        return sx1262_status();
+    }
+}
+
+static void sx1262_set_cs(void *opaque, int level)
+{
+    M5Sx1262State *s = opaque;
+    if (level) {
+        s->mode = SX_IDLE;
+        s->response_pos = 0;
+        s->response_len = 0;
+    }
+}
+
+static const M5SpiSlaveOps sx1262_ops = {
+    .set_cs = sx1262_set_cs,
+    .transfer = sx1262_transfer,
+};
+
+void m5_sx1262_attach(DeviceState *spi, int cs_pin)
+{
+    M5Sx1262State *s = g_new0(M5Sx1262State, 1);
+    s->ops = &sx1262_ops;
+    esp32s3_gpspi_attach_spi_slave(spi, &sx1262_ops, s, cs_pin);
+    esp32_gpio_set_input(21, 0); /* SX_BUSY: command processor ready */
+    esp32_gpio_force_line(21, 0);
+    esp32_gpio_set_input(5, 0);  /* DIO1 idle */
+}

new file mode 100644
index 0000000..e6c68cf

+++ b/hw/m5stopwatch/m5atoms3r.c
@@ -0,0 +1,80 @@
+/*
+ * M5Stack AtomS3R board glue: ESP32-S3-PICO-1-N8R8 + ST7735S
+ * 128x128 SPI panel, BMI270 IMU and one GPIO button.
+ *
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
+ */
+#include "qemu/osdep.h"
+#include "hw/xtensa/esp32s3_intc.h"
+#include "hw/dma/esp_gdma.h"
+#include "hw/qdev-properties.h"
+#include "qapi/error.h"
+#include "m5stopwatch.h"
+
+void m5atoms3r_board_init(MemoryRegion *sys_mem, DeviceState *intmatrix,
+                          ESPGdmaState *gdma)
+{
+    m5stopwatch_sens_init(sys_mem);
+    m5stopwatch_regi2c_init(sys_mem);
+    m5stopwatch_fe_init(sys_mem);
+    m5stopwatch_phy_init(sys_mem);
+
+    DeviceState *i2c0 = qdev_new(TYPE_ESP32S3_I2C);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c0), &error_fatal);
+    MemoryRegion *i2c0_mr = sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c0), 0);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60013000, i2c0_mr, 1);
+
+    DeviceState *i2c1 = qdev_new(TYPE_ESP32S3_I2C);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c1), &error_fatal);
+    MemoryRegion *i2c1_mr = sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c1), 0);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60027000, i2c1_mr, 1);
+    sysbus_connect_irq(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c0), 0,
+                       qdev_get_gpio_in(intmatrix, ETS_I2C_EXT0_INTR_SOURCE));
+    sysbus_connect_irq(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c1), 0,
+                       qdev_get_gpio_in(intmatrix, ETS_I2C_EXT1_INTR_SOURCE));
+
+    I2CBus *bus = (I2CBus *)qdev_get_child_bus(i2c0, "i2c");
+    esp32s3_i2c_set_bus(i2c1, bus);
+
+    static const struct { const char *model; uint8_t addr; } devs[] = {
+        { "lp5562", 0x30 },
+        { "bmm150", 0x10 },
+        { "bmi270", 0x68 },
+    };
+    for (int i = 0; i < ARRAY_SIZE(devs); i++) {
+        DeviceState *d = qdev_new(TYPE_M5SW_I2C_DEV);
+        qdev_prop_set_string(d, "model", devs[i].model);
+        qdev_prop_set_uint8(d, "address", devs[i].addr);
+        object_property_add_child(qdev_get_machine(), devs[i].model, OBJECT(d));
+        qdev_realize_and_unref(d, (BusState *)bus, &error_fatal);
+    }
+    m5stopwatch_soft_i2c_init(bus);
+
+    DeviceState *spi = qdev_new(TYPE_ESP32S3_GPSPI);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(spi), &error_fatal);
+    MemoryRegion *spi_mr = sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(spi), 0);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60025000, spi_mr, 1);
+    MemoryRegion *spi2_alias = g_new(MemoryRegion, 1);
+    memory_region_init_alias(spi2_alias, NULL, "m5atoms3r.spi2-alias",
+                             spi_mr, 0, memory_region_size(spi_mr));
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60024000, spi2_alias, 1);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_connect_irqs(spi, intmatrix, ETS_SPI2_INTR_SOURCE,
+                                ETS_SPI3_INTR_SOURCE);
+
+    DeviceState *panel = qdev_new(TYPE_M5_SPI_PANEL);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "mem-width", 132);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "mem-height", 132);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "vis-width", 128);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "vis-height", 128);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "offset-x", 2);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "offset-y", 1);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "key-a-pin", 41);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "key-b-pin", 41);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "read-id", 0x00007683);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "glass-rotation", 0);
+    qdev_prop_set_bit(panel, "apply-madctl", false);
+    qdev_prop_set_bit(panel, "invert", true);
+    qdev_realize_and_unref(panel, NULL, &error_fatal);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_attach(spi, gdma, NULL, M5_SPI_PANEL(panel), GDMA_SPI3);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_set_dc_pin(spi, 42);
+}

+/*
+ * M5Stack Cardputer-ADV board glue: ESP32-S3FN8 + ST7789 240x135,
+ * TCA8418 keyboard controller and BMI270 IMU.
+ *
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
+ */
+#include "qemu/osdep.h"
+#include "hw/xtensa/esp32s3_intc.h"
+#include "hw/dma/esp_gdma.h"
+#include "hw/qdev-properties.h"
+#include "qapi/error.h"
+#include "m5stopwatch.h"
+
+void m5cardputeradv_board_init(MemoryRegion *sys_mem, DeviceState *intmatrix,
+                               ESPGdmaState *gdma)
+{
+    m5stopwatch_sens_init(sys_mem);
+    m5stopwatch_regi2c_init(sys_mem);
+    m5stopwatch_fe_init(sys_mem);
+    m5stopwatch_phy_init(sys_mem);
+
+    DeviceState *i2c0 = qdev_new(TYPE_ESP32S3_I2C);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c0), &error_fatal);
+    MemoryRegion *i2c0_mr = sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c0), 0);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60013000, i2c0_mr, 1);
+
+    DeviceState *i2c1 = qdev_new(TYPE_ESP32S3_I2C);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c1), &error_fatal);
+    MemoryRegion *i2c1_mr = sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c1), 0);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60027000, i2c1_mr, 1);
+    sysbus_connect_irq(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c0), 0,
+                       qdev_get_gpio_in(intmatrix, ETS_I2C_EXT0_INTR_SOURCE));
+    sysbus_connect_irq(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c1), 0,
+                       qdev_get_gpio_in(intmatrix, ETS_I2C_EXT1_INTR_SOURCE));
+
+    I2CBus *bus = (I2CBus *)qdev_get_child_bus(i2c0, "i2c");
+    esp32s3_i2c_set_bus(i2c1, bus);
+
+    static const struct { const char *model; uint8_t addr; } devs[] = {
+        { "tca8418", 0x34 },
+        { "bmi270", 0x69 },
+        { "es8311", 0x18 },
+    };
+    for (int i = 0; i < ARRAY_SIZE(devs); i++) {
+        DeviceState *d = qdev_new(TYPE_M5SW_I2C_DEV);
+        qdev_prop_set_string(d, "model", devs[i].model);
+        qdev_prop_set_uint8(d, "address", devs[i].addr);
+        object_property_add_child(qdev_get_machine(), devs[i].model, OBJECT(d));
+        qdev_realize_and_unref(d, (BusState *)bus, &error_fatal);
+    }
+
+    DeviceState *spi = qdev_new(TYPE_ESP32S3_GPSPI);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(spi), &error_fatal);
+    MemoryRegion *spi_mr = sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(spi), 0);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60025000, spi_mr, 1);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_connect_irqs(spi, intmatrix, ETS_SPI2_INTR_SOURCE,
+                                ETS_SPI3_INTR_SOURCE);
+
+    DeviceState *panel = qdev_new(TYPE_M5_SPI_PANEL);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "mem-width", 320);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "mem-height", 240);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "vis-width", 240);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "vis-height", 135);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "offset-x", 40);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "offset-y", 53);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "read-id", 0x00000081);
+    qdev_prop_set_bit(panel, "apply-madctl", false);
+    qdev_prop_set_bit(panel, "keyboard", true);
+    qdev_prop_set_bit(panel, "invert", true);
+    qdev_realize_and_unref(panel, NULL, &error_fatal);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_attach(spi, gdma, NULL, M5_SPI_PANEL(panel), GDMA_SPI3);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_set_dc_pin(spi, 34);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_set_panel_cs_pin(spi, 37);
+
+    DeviceState *sd_spi = qdev_new(TYPE_ESP32S3_GPSPI);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(sd_spi), &error_fatal);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(
+        sys_mem, 0x60024000,
+        sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(sd_spi), 0), 1);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_connect_irqs(sd_spi, intmatrix, ETS_SPI2_INTR_SOURCE,
+                                ETS_SPI3_INTR_SOURCE);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_attach(sd_spi, gdma, NULL, M5_SPI_PANEL(panel), GDMA_SPI2);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_set_dc_pin(sd_spi, 34);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_set_panel_cs_pin(sd_spi, 37);
+    m5_spi_sd_attach(sd_spi, 12);
+    m5_st25r3916_spi_attach(spi, 6, 4);
+    m5_st25r3916_spi_attach(sd_spi, 6, 4);
+    m5_cc1101_spi_attach(spi, 5, 15);
+    m5_cc1101_spi_attach(sd_spi, 5, 15);

diff --git a/hw/m5stopwatch/m5core2.c b/hw/m5stopwatch/m5core2.c
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..98c4352
--- /dev/null
+++ b/hw/m5stopwatch/m5core2.c
@@ -0,0 +1,74 @@
+/*
+ * M5Stack Core2 board glue: ESP32 LX6 + ILI9342 320x240 SPI panel,
+ * AXP192 PMIC and FT5x06-compatible touch.
+ *
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
+ */
+#include "qemu/osdep.h"
+#include "hw/xtensa/esp32.h"
+#include "hw/qdev-properties.h"
+#include "qapi/error.h"
+#include "m5stopwatch.h"
+
+void m5core2_board_init(Esp32SocState *s)
+{
+    I2CBus *bus0 = (I2CBus *)qdev_get_child_bus(DEVICE(&s->i2c[0]), "i2c");
+    I2CBus *bus1 = (I2CBus *)qdev_get_child_bus(DEVICE(&s->i2c[1]), "i2c");
+    const char *pmic_override = getenv("M5SW_CORE2_PMIC");
+    bool legacy_axp192 = pmic_override && !strcmp(pmic_override, "axp192");
+
+    DeviceState *axp = qdev_new(TYPE_M5SW_I2C_DEV);
+    qdev_prop_set_string(axp, "model", legacy_axp192 ? "axp192" : "axp2101");
+    qdev_prop_set_uint8(axp, "address", 0x34);
+    object_property_add_child(qdev_get_machine(),
+                              legacy_axp192 ? "axp192" : "axp2101",
+                              OBJECT(axp));
+    qdev_realize_and_unref(axp, (BusState *)bus0, &error_fatal);
+
+    if (!legacy_axp192) {
+        for (uint8_t address = 0x40; address <= 0x41; address++) {
+            DeviceState *ina = qdev_new(TYPE_M5SW_I2C_DEV);
+            char *path = g_strdup_printf("ina3221-%02x", address);
+
+            qdev_prop_set_string(ina, "model", "ina3221");
+            qdev_prop_set_uint8(ina, "address", address);
+            object_property_add_child(qdev_get_machine(), path, OBJECT(ina));
+            qdev_realize_and_unref(ina, (BusState *)bus0, &error_fatal);
+            g_free(path);
+        }
+    }
+
+    static const struct { const char *model; uint8_t addr; const char *path; } devs[] = {
+        { "ft5x06", 0x38, "core2-ft5x06" },
+        { "mpu6886", 0x68, "mpu6886" },
+        { "pcf8563", 0x51, "pcf8563" },
+    };
+    for (int i = 0; i < ARRAY_SIZE(devs); i++) {
+        DeviceState *d = qdev_new(TYPE_M5SW_I2C_DEV);
+        qdev_prop_set_string(d, "model", devs[i].model);
+        qdev_prop_set_uint8(d, "address", devs[i].addr);
+        object_property_add_child(qdev_get_machine(), devs[i].path, OBJECT(d));
+        qdev_realize_and_unref(d, (BusState *)bus1, &error_fatal);
+    }
+
+    m5stopwatch_soft_i2c_init_pins(bus0, 21, 22);
+    esp32_gpio_set_input(39, 1);
+
+    DeviceState *panel = qdev_new(TYPE_M5_SPI_PANEL);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "mem-width", 320);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "mem-height", 240);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "vis-width", 320);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "vis-height", 240);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "glass-rotation", 0);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "offset-x", 0);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "offset-y", 0);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "read-id", 0x000000e3);
+    qdev_prop_set_bit(panel, "apply-madctl", false);
+    qdev_prop_set_bit(panel, "touch", true);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "touch-int-pin", 39);
+    qdev_prop_set_bit(panel, "immediate-update", true);
+    qdev_prop_set_bit(panel, "invert", true);
+    object_property_add_child(qdev_get_machine(), "m5-panel", OBJECT(panel));
+    qdev_realize_and_unref(panel, NULL, &error_fatal);
+    esp32_spi_attach_m5_panel(DEVICE(&s->spi[3]), panel, 15);
+}

+/*
+ * M5Stack CoreS3 board glue: ESP32-S3 + ILI9342 320x240 SPI panel,
+ * AXP2101 PMIC, AW9523 GPIO expander, GC0308 camera and
+ * FT5x06-compatible touch.
+ *
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
+ */
+#include "qemu/osdep.h"
+#include "hw/xtensa/esp32s3_intc.h"
+#include "hw/dma/esp_gdma.h"
+#include "hw/qdev-properties.h"
+#include "qapi/error.h"
+#include "m5stopwatch.h"
+
+void m5cores3_board_init(MemoryRegion *sys_mem, DeviceState *intmatrix,
+                         ESPGdmaState *gdma)
+{
+    m5stopwatch_sens_init(sys_mem);
+    m5stopwatch_regi2c_init(sys_mem);
+    m5stopwatch_fe_init(sys_mem);
+    m5stopwatch_phy_init(sys_mem);
+
+    DeviceState *i2c0 = qdev_new(TYPE_ESP32S3_I2C);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c0), &error_fatal);
+    MemoryRegion *i2c0_mr = sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c0), 0);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60013000, i2c0_mr, 1);
+
+    DeviceState *i2c1 = qdev_new(TYPE_ESP32S3_I2C);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c1), &error_fatal);
+    MemoryRegion *i2c1_mr = sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c1), 0);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60027000, i2c1_mr, 1);
+    sysbus_connect_irq(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c0), 0,
+                       qdev_get_gpio_in(intmatrix, ETS_I2C_EXT0_INTR_SOURCE));
+    sysbus_connect_irq(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c1), 0,
+                       qdev_get_gpio_in(intmatrix, ETS_I2C_EXT1_INTR_SOURCE));
+
+    I2CBus *bus = (I2CBus *)qdev_get_child_bus(i2c0, "i2c");
+    esp32s3_i2c_set_bus(i2c1, bus);
+
+    static const struct { const char *model; uint8_t addr; } devs[] = {
+        { "axp2101", 0x34 },
+        { "aw9523", 0x58 },
+        { "bmm150", 0x10 },
+        { "gc0308", 0x21 },
+        { "ltr553", 0x23 },
+        { "aw88298", 0x36 },
+        { "ft5x06", 0x38 },
+        { "pcf8563", 0x51 },
+        { "es7210", 0x40 },
+        { "bmi270", 0x69 },
+        { "rx8130", 0x32 },
+        { "es8311", 0x18 },
+    };
+    for (int i = 0; i < ARRAY_SIZE(devs); i++) {
+        DeviceState *d = qdev_new(TYPE_M5SW_I2C_DEV);
+        qdev_prop_set_string(d, "model", devs[i].model);
+        qdev_prop_set_uint8(d, "address", devs[i].addr);
+        object_property_add_child(qdev_get_machine(), devs[i].model, OBJECT(d));
+        qdev_realize_and_unref(d, (BusState *)bus, &error_fatal);
+    }
+    m5stopwatch_soft_i2c_init_pins(bus, 12, 11);
+
+    DeviceState *cam = qdev_new(TYPE_M5_LCD_CAM);
+    m5_lcd_cam_attach(cam, gdma, 320, 240);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(cam), &error_fatal);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(
+        sys_mem, 0x60041000,
+        sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(cam), 0), 1);
+    sysbus_connect_irq(
+        SYS_BUS_DEVICE(cam), 0,
+        qdev_get_gpio_in(intmatrix, ETS_LCD_CAM_INTR_SOURCE));
+
+    DeviceState *spi = qdev_new(TYPE_ESP32S3_GPSPI);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(spi), &error_fatal);
+    MemoryRegion *spi_mr = sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(spi), 0);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60025000, spi_mr, 1);
+    MemoryRegion *spi2_alias = g_new(MemoryRegion, 1);
+    memory_region_init_alias(spi2_alias, NULL, "m5cores3.spi2-alias",
+                             spi_mr, 0, memory_region_size(spi_mr));
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60024000, spi2_alias, 1);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_connect_irqs(spi, intmatrix, ETS_SPI2_INTR_SOURCE,
+                                ETS_SPI3_INTR_SOURCE);
+
+    DeviceState *panel = qdev_new(TYPE_M5_SPI_PANEL);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "mem-width", 320);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "mem-height", 240);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "vis-width", 320);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "vis-height", 240);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "offset-x", 0);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "offset-y", 0);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "key-a-pin", 0);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "key-b-pin", 0);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "read-id", 0x000000e3);
+    qdev_prop_set_bit(panel, "apply-madctl", false);
+    qdev_prop_set_bit(panel, "touch", true);
+    qdev_prop_set_bit(panel, "invert", true);
+    qdev_realize_and_unref(panel, NULL, &error_fatal);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_attach(spi, gdma, NULL, M5_SPI_PANEL(panel), GDMA_SPI3);

+/*
+ * M5Stack PaperColor board glue: ESP32-S3R8, ED2208 Spectra 6
+ * 400x600 e-paper, M5PM1 and three GPIO keys.
+ *
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later

+#include "hw/xtensa/esp32s3_intc.h"

+#include "qapi/error.h"
+#include "m5stopwatch.h"

+void m5papercolor_board_init(MemoryRegion *sys_mem, DeviceState *intmatrix,
+                             ESPGdmaState *gdma)
+{
+    m5stopwatch_sens_init(sys_mem);
+    m5stopwatch_regi2c_init(sys_mem);
+    m5stopwatch_fe_init(sys_mem);

+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60013000, i2c0_mr, 1);
+
+    DeviceState *i2c1 = qdev_new(TYPE_ESP32S3_I2C);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c1), &error_fatal);
+    MemoryRegion *i2c1_mr = sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c1), 0);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60027000, i2c1_mr, 1);
+    sysbus_connect_irq(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c0), 0,
+                       qdev_get_gpio_in(intmatrix, ETS_I2C_EXT0_INTR_SOURCE));
+    sysbus_connect_irq(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c1), 0,
+                       qdev_get_gpio_in(intmatrix, ETS_I2C_EXT1_INTR_SOURCE));

+    esp32s3_i2c_set_bus(i2c1, bus);
+
+    static const struct { const char *model; uint8_t addr; } devs[] = {
+        { "m5pm1", 0x6E },
+        { "rx8130", 0x32 },
+        { "es8311", 0x18 },
+        { "es7210", 0x40 },
+        { "sht40", 0x44 },
+    };
+    for (int i = 0; i < ARRAY_SIZE(devs); i++) {

+    esp32s3_gpspi_connect_irqs(spi, intmatrix, ETS_SPI2_INTR_SOURCE,

+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "native-width", 400);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "native-height", 600);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "out-width", 400);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "out-height", 600);
+    qdev_prop_set_bit(panel, "color", true);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "busy-pin", 11);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "key-a-pin", 10);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "key-b-pin", 9);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "key-c-pin", 1);
+    qdev_realize_and_unref(panel, NULL, &error_fatal);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_attach_epd(spi, gdma, M5_EPD_PANEL(panel), GDMA_SPI2);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_set_dc_pin(spi, 43);

+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(sd_spi), &error_fatal);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(
+        sys_mem, 0x60025000,
+        sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(sd_spi), 0), 1);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_connect_irqs(sd_spi, intmatrix, ETS_SPI2_INTR_SOURCE,
+                                ETS_SPI3_INTR_SOURCE);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_attach(sd_spi, gdma, NULL, NULL, GDMA_SPI3);
+    m5_spi_sd_attach(sd_spi, 47);
+}

+/*
+ * M5Stack PaperMono board glue: ESP32-S3R8, SSD1677 480x800 e-paper,
+ * M5PM1/M5IOE1, FT5x06 touch and NFC identity.
+ *
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
+ */
+#include "qemu/osdep.h"
+#include "hw/xtensa/esp32s3_intc.h"
+#include "hw/dma/esp_gdma.h"
+#include "hw/qdev-properties.h"
+#include "qapi/error.h"
+#include "m5stopwatch.h"
+
+void m5papermono_board_init(MemoryRegion *sys_mem, DeviceState *intmatrix,
+                            ESPGdmaState *gdma)
+{
+    m5stopwatch_sens_init(sys_mem);
+    m5stopwatch_regi2c_init(sys_mem);
+    m5stopwatch_fe_init(sys_mem);
+    m5stopwatch_phy_init(sys_mem);
+
+    DeviceState *i2c0 = qdev_new(TYPE_ESP32S3_I2C);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c0), &error_fatal);
+    MemoryRegion *i2c0_mr = sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c0), 0);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60013000, i2c0_mr, 1);
+
+    DeviceState *i2c1 = qdev_new(TYPE_ESP32S3_I2C);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c1), &error_fatal);
+    MemoryRegion *i2c1_mr = sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c1), 0);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60027000, i2c1_mr, 1);
+    sysbus_connect_irq(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c0), 0,
+                       qdev_get_gpio_in(intmatrix, ETS_I2C_EXT0_INTR_SOURCE));
+    sysbus_connect_irq(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c1), 0,
+                       qdev_get_gpio_in(intmatrix, ETS_I2C_EXT1_INTR_SOURCE));
+
+    I2CBus *bus = (I2CBus *)qdev_get_child_bus(i2c0, "i2c");
+    esp32s3_i2c_set_bus(i2c1, bus);
+
+    static const struct { const char *model; uint8_t addr; } devs[] = {
+        { "m5pm1", 0x6E },
+        { "m5ioe1", 0x4F },
+        { "ft5x06", 0x38 },
+        { "rx8130", 0x32 },
+        { "bmi270", 0x68 },
+        { "st25r3916", 0x50 },
+    };
+    for (int i = 0; i < ARRAY_SIZE(devs); i++) {
+        DeviceState *d = qdev_new(TYPE_M5SW_I2C_DEV);
+        qdev_prop_set_string(d, "model", devs[i].model);
+        qdev_prop_set_uint8(d, "address", devs[i].addr);
+        object_property_add_child(qdev_get_machine(), devs[i].model, OBJECT(d));
+        qdev_realize_and_unref(d, (BusState *)bus, &error_fatal);
+    }
+    m5stopwatch_soft_i2c_init_pins(bus, 47, 48);
+
+    DeviceState *spi2 = qdev_new(TYPE_ESP32S3_GPSPI);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(spi2), &error_fatal);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(
+        sys_mem, 0x60024000,
+        sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(spi2), 0), 1);
+    sysbus_connect_irq(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(spi2), 0,
+                       qdev_get_gpio_in(intmatrix, ETS_SPI2_INTR_SOURCE));
+
+    DeviceState *spi3 = qdev_new(TYPE_ESP32S3_GPSPI);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(spi3), &error_fatal);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(
+        sys_mem, 0x60025000,
+        sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(spi3), 0), 1);
+    sysbus_connect_irq(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(spi3), 0,
+                       qdev_get_gpio_in(intmatrix, ETS_SPI3_INTR_SOURCE));
+
+    DeviceState *panel = qdev_new(TYPE_M5_EPD_PANEL);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "native-width", 800);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "native-height", 480);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "out-width", 480);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "out-height", 800);
+    qdev_prop_set_bit(panel, "color", false);
+    qdev_prop_set_bit(panel, "rotate", true);
+    qdev_prop_set_bit(panel, "touch", true);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "touch-int-pin", 4);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "key-a-pin", 2);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "key-b-pin", 3);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "key-c-pin", UINT32_MAX);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "busy-pin", 18);
+    qdev_prop_set_bit(panel, "busy-ready-level", false);
+    qdev_realize_and_unref(panel, NULL, &error_fatal);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_attach_epd(spi2, gdma, M5_EPD_PANEL(panel), GDMA_SPI2);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_set_dc_pin(spi2, 17);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_attach(spi3, gdma, NULL, NULL, GDMA_SPI3);
+    m5_sx1262_attach(spi3, 41);
+}

+/*
+ * M5Stack StackChan board glue: ESP32-S3 + ILI9342 320x240,
+ * AXP2101/AW9523/FT5x06 touch, GC0308 camera identity and M5IOE1.
+ *
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later

+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60013000, i2c0_mr, 1);
+
+    DeviceState *i2c1 = qdev_new(TYPE_ESP32S3_I2C);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c1), &error_fatal);
+    MemoryRegion *i2c1_mr = sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c1), 0);

+
+    I2CBus *bus = (I2CBus *)qdev_get_child_bus(i2c0, "i2c");
+    esp32s3_i2c_set_bus(i2c1, bus);
+
+    static const struct { const char *model; uint8_t addr; } devs[] = {
+        { "axp2101", 0x34 },
+        { "aw9523", 0x58 },
+        { "bmm150", 0x10 },
+        { "ft5x06", 0x38 },
+        { "gc0308", 0x21 },
+        { "aw88298", 0x36 },
+        { "es7210", 0x40 },
+        { "m5ioe1_v4", 0x6F },
+        { "bmi270", 0x69 },
+        { "rx8130", 0x32 },
+        { "ina226", 0x41 },
+        { "st25r3916", 0x50 },
+        { "si12t", 0x68 },

+    m5_scs_servo_attach(m5soc_get_uart_device(1));
+

+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(cam), &error_fatal);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(
+        sys_mem, 0x60041000,
+        sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(cam), 0), 1);
+    sysbus_connect_irq(
+        SYS_BUS_DEVICE(cam), 0,
+        qdev_get_gpio_in(intmatrix, ETS_LCD_CAM_INTR_SOURCE));
+
+    DeviceState *spi = qdev_new(TYPE_ESP32S3_GPSPI);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(spi), &error_fatal);
+    MemoryRegion *spi_mr = sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(spi), 0);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60025000, spi_mr, 1);
+    MemoryRegion *spi2_alias = g_new(MemoryRegion, 1);
+    memory_region_init_alias(spi2_alias, NULL, "m5stackchan.spi2-alias",
+                             spi_mr, 0, memory_region_size(spi_mr));
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60024000, spi2_alias, 1);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_connect_irqs(spi, intmatrix, ETS_SPI2_INTR_SOURCE,
+                                ETS_SPI3_INTR_SOURCE);
+
+    DeviceState *panel = qdev_new(TYPE_M5_SPI_PANEL);

+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "mem-height", 240);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "vis-width", 320);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "vis-height", 240);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "offset-x", 0);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "offset-y", 0);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "read-id", 0x000000e3);
+    qdev_prop_set_bit(panel, "apply-madctl", false);
+    qdev_prop_set_bit(panel, "touch", true);
+    qdev_prop_set_bit(panel, "invert", true);

+/*
+ * M5Stack StamPLC board glue: ESP32-S3FN8 + ST7789 240x135,
+ * PI4IOE5V6408 industrial I/O expander and three front-panel keys.
+ *
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
+ */

+#include "hw/xtensa/esp32s3_intc.h"
+#include "hw/dma/esp_gdma.h"
+#include "hw/qdev-properties.h"
+#include "qapi/error.h"
+#include "m5stopwatch.h"
+
+void m5stamplc_board_init(MemoryRegion *sys_mem, DeviceState *intmatrix,
+                          ESPGdmaState *gdma)
+{
+    m5stopwatch_sens_init(sys_mem);
+    m5stopwatch_regi2c_init(sys_mem);
+    m5stopwatch_fe_init(sys_mem);
+    m5stopwatch_phy_init(sys_mem);

+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60013000, i2c0_mr, 1);
+
+    DeviceState *i2c1 = qdev_new(TYPE_ESP32S3_I2C);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c1), &error_fatal);
+    MemoryRegion *i2c1_mr = sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c1), 0);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60027000, i2c1_mr, 1);

+                       qdev_get_gpio_in(intmatrix, ETS_I2C_EXT0_INTR_SOURCE));
+    sysbus_connect_irq(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c1), 0,
+                       qdev_get_gpio_in(intmatrix, ETS_I2C_EXT1_INTR_SOURCE));
+
+    I2CBus *bus = (I2CBus *)qdev_get_child_bus(i2c0, "i2c");
+    esp32s3_i2c_set_bus(i2c1, bus);
+
+    static const struct { const char *model; uint8_t addr; } devs[] = {
+        { "ina226", 0x40 },
+        { "pi4ioe", 0x43 },
+        { "lm75", 0x48 },
+        { "aw9523", 0x59 },
+        { "rx8130", 0x32 },
+    };
+    for (int i = 0; i < ARRAY_SIZE(devs); i++) {
+        DeviceState *d = qdev_new(TYPE_M5SW_I2C_DEV);
+        qdev_prop_set_string(d, "model", devs[i].model);
+        qdev_prop_set_uint8(d, "address", devs[i].addr);
+        object_property_add_child(qdev_get_machine(), devs[i].model, OBJECT(d));
+        qdev_realize_and_unref(d, (BusState *)bus, &error_fatal);
+    }
+

+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(spi), &error_fatal);
+    MemoryRegion *spi_mr = sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(spi), 0);

+    MemoryRegion *spi2_alias = g_new(MemoryRegion, 1);

+                             spi_mr, 0, memory_region_size(spi_mr));

+    DeviceState *panel = qdev_new(TYPE_M5_SPI_PANEL);

+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "mem-height", 240);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "vis-width", 240);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "vis-height", 135);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "offset-x", 40);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "offset-y", 53);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "read-id", 0x00000081);
+    qdev_prop_set_bit(panel, "apply-madctl", false);
+    qdev_prop_set_bit(panel, "plc-keys", true);
+    qdev_prop_set_bit(panel, "invert", true);
+    qdev_realize_and_unref(panel, NULL, &error_fatal);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_attach(spi, gdma, NULL, M5_SPI_PANEL(panel), GDMA_SPI3);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_set_dc_pin(spi, 6);
+
+    /* PWR-485 is UART2: TX/BOOT G0, RX G39, direction G46. */
+    m5_uart_echo_attach(m5soc_get_uart_device(2), "M5SW_RS485_ECHO");
+}

+/*
+ * M5Stack StickS3 board glue: ESP32-S3 + ST7789 135x240 SPI panel,
+ * M5PM1 PMIC, BMI270 IMU and two GPIO buttons.
+ *
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
+ */
+#include "qemu/osdep.h"
+#include "hw/xtensa/esp32s3_intc.h"
+#include "hw/dma/esp_gdma.h"
+#include "hw/qdev-properties.h"
+#include "qapi/error.h"
+#include "m5stopwatch.h"
+
+void m5sticks3_board_init(MemoryRegion *sys_mem, DeviceState *intmatrix,
+                          ESPGdmaState *gdma)
+{
+    m5stopwatch_sens_init(sys_mem);
+    m5stopwatch_regi2c_init(sys_mem);
+    m5stopwatch_fe_init(sys_mem);
+    m5stopwatch_phy_init(sys_mem);
+
+    DeviceState *i2c0 = qdev_new(TYPE_ESP32S3_I2C);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c0), &error_fatal);
+    MemoryRegion *i2c0_mr = sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c0), 0);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60013000, i2c0_mr, 1);
+
+    DeviceState *i2c1 = qdev_new(TYPE_ESP32S3_I2C);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c1), &error_fatal);
+    MemoryRegion *i2c1_mr = sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c1), 0);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60027000, i2c1_mr, 1);
+    sysbus_connect_irq(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c0), 0,
+                       qdev_get_gpio_in(intmatrix, ETS_I2C_EXT0_INTR_SOURCE));
+    sysbus_connect_irq(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c1), 0,
+                       qdev_get_gpio_in(intmatrix, ETS_I2C_EXT1_INTR_SOURCE));
+
+    I2CBus *bus = (I2CBus *)qdev_get_child_bus(i2c0, "i2c");
+    esp32s3_i2c_set_bus(i2c1, bus);
+
+    static const struct { const char *model; uint8_t addr; } devs[] = {
+        { "m5pm1", 0x6E },
+        { "bmi270", 0x68 },
+        { "es8311", 0x18 },
+    };
+    for (int i = 0; i < ARRAY_SIZE(devs); i++) {
+        DeviceState *d = qdev_new(TYPE_M5SW_I2C_DEV);
+        qdev_prop_set_string(d, "model", devs[i].model);

+    }
+    m5stopwatch_soft_i2c_init(bus);

+    DeviceState *spi = qdev_new(TYPE_ESP32S3_GPSPI);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(spi), &error_fatal);
+    MemoryRegion *spi_mr = sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(spi), 0);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60025000, spi_mr, 1);
+    MemoryRegion *spi2_alias = g_new(MemoryRegion, 1);
+    memory_region_init_alias(spi2_alias, NULL, "m5sticks3.spi2-alias",
+                             spi_mr, 0, memory_region_size(spi_mr));
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60024000, spi2_alias, 1);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_connect_irqs(spi, intmatrix, ETS_SPI2_INTR_SOURCE,
+                                ETS_SPI3_INTR_SOURCE);
+
+    DeviceState *panel = qdev_new(TYPE_M5_SPI_PANEL);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "mem-width", 240);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "mem-height", 320);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "vis-width", 135);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "vis-height", 240);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "offset-x", 52);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "offset-y", 40);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "key-a-pin", 11);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "key-b-pin", 12);
+    qdev_prop_set_bit(panel, "invert", true);
+    qdev_realize_and_unref(panel, NULL, &error_fatal);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_attach(spi, gdma, NULL, M5_SPI_PANEL(panel), GDMA_SPI3);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_set_dc_pin(spi, 45);
+}
+
+void m5_board_init(MemoryRegion *sys_mem, DeviceState *intmatrix,
+                   ESPGdmaState *gdma)
+{
+    m5_i2s_board_init(sys_mem, intmatrix, gdma);
+    switch (m5_board) {
+    case M5_BOARD_STOPWATCH:
+        m5stopwatch_board_init(sys_mem, intmatrix, gdma);
+        break;
+    case M5_BOARD_STICKS3:
+        m5sticks3_board_init(sys_mem, intmatrix, gdma);
+        break;
+    case M5_BOARD_ATOMS3R:
+        m5atoms3r_board_init(sys_mem, intmatrix, gdma);
+        break;
+    case M5_BOARD_CORES3:
+        m5cores3_board_init(sys_mem, intmatrix, gdma);
+        break;
+    case M5_BOARD_CARDPUTER_ADV:
+        m5cardputeradv_board_init(sys_mem, intmatrix, gdma);
+        break;
+    case M5_BOARD_STAMPLC:
+        m5stamplc_board_init(sys_mem, intmatrix, gdma);
+        break;
+    case M5_BOARD_STACKCHAN:
+        m5stackchan_board_init(sys_mem, intmatrix, gdma);
+        break;
+    case M5_BOARD_PAPERMONO:
+        m5papermono_board_init(sys_mem, intmatrix, gdma);
+        break;
+    case M5_BOARD_PAPERCOLOR:
+        m5papercolor_board_init(sys_mem, intmatrix, gdma);
+        break;
+    default:
+        break;
+    }

 static M5swI2CDev *g_touch;
+static M5swI2CDev *g_ft_touch;
 static M5swI2CDev *g_pm1;

+static unsigned g_key_event_count;
+static uint8_t g_plc_keys = 0x07;
+
+static uint8_t aw9523_input_read(M5swI2CDev *s, int bank)
+{
+    uint8_t config = s->regs[0x04 + bank];
+    uint8_t external = (s->aw9523_input_bits >> (bank * 8)) & 0xff;
+    uint8_t output = s->regs[0x02 + bank];
+
+    return (config & external) | (~config & output);
+}
+
+static void aw9523_update_irq(M5swI2CDev *s)
+{
+    uint8_t level = s->aw9523_irq_bits != 0;
+
+    if (s->parent_obj.address == 0x58) {
+        esp32_gpio_force_line(21, !level);
+    }
+}
+
+static void aw9523_set_input_bits(M5swI2CDev *s, int64_t value)
+{
+    uint16_t old = s->aw9523_input_bits;
+    uint16_t now = value;
+    uint16_t changed = old ^ now;
+    uint16_t enabled = s->regs[0x06] | (s->regs[0x07] << 8);
+
+    s->aw9523_input_bits = now;
+    s->aw9523_irq_bits |= changed & enabled;
+    aw9523_update_irq(s);
+}
+
+static uint8_t pi4ioe_input_read(M5swI2CDev *s)
+{
+    uint8_t direction = s->regs[0x03];
+    uint8_t external = s->pi4ioe_input_bits;
+
+    /*
+     * StamPLC uses P0..P2 for active-low front-panel keys while the
+     * remaining inputs are provided by the industrial input bank.
+     */
+    if (s->parent_obj.address == 0x43) {
+        external = (external & 0xf8) | g_plc_keys;
+    }
+    return external & ~direction;
+}
+
+static void pi4ioe_update_irq(M5swI2CDev *s)
+{
+    if (s->parent_obj.address == 0x43) {
+        esp32_gpio_force_line(21, s->pi4ioe_irq_bits == 0);
+    }
+}
+
+static void pi4ioe_set_input_bits(M5swI2CDev *s, int64_t value)
+{
+    uint8_t now = value;
+    uint8_t default_state = s->regs[0x09];
+    uint8_t active;
+
+    s->pi4ioe_input_bits = now;
+    active = pi4ioe_input_read(s);
+    s->pi4ioe_irq_bits |= (active ^ default_state) & ~s->regs[0x03];
+    pi4ioe_update_irq(s);
+}
+
+static uint8_t m5ioe1_input_read(M5swI2CDev *s, int bank)
+{
+    uint16_t mode = s->regs[0x03] | (s->regs[0x04] << 8);
+    uint16_t output = s->regs[0x05] | (s->regs[0x06] << 8);
+    uint16_t external = s->m5ioe1_input_bits;
+
+    return ((mode & output) | (~mode & external)) >> (bank * 8);
+}
+
+static void m5ioe1_set_input_bits(M5swI2CDev *s, int64_t value)
+{
+    uint16_t old = s->m5ioe1_input_bits;
+    uint16_t now = value & 0x3fff;
+    uint16_t changed = old ^ now;
+    uint16_t enabled = s->regs[0x0d] | (s->regs[0x0e] << 8);
+    uint16_t polarity = s->regs[0x0f] | (s->regs[0x10] << 8);
+    uint16_t active = (changed & now & polarity)
+                      | (changed & ~now & ~polarity);
+
+    s->m5ioe1_input_bits = now;
+    s->regs[0x11] |= active & enabled;
+    s->regs[0x12] |= (active & enabled) >> 8;
+}

+void m5sw_ft_touch_set(int pressed, int x, int y)
+{
+    m5sw_ft_touch_set_pin(pressed, x, y, 21);
+}
+
+void m5sw_ft_touch_set_pin(int pressed, int x, int y, int int_pin)
+{
+    if (!g_ft_touch) {
+        return;
+    }
+    g_ft_touch->regs[0x02] = pressed ? 1 : 0;
+    g_ft_touch->regs[0x03] = (x >> 8) & 0x0f;
+    g_ft_touch->regs[0x04] = x & 0xff;
+    g_ft_touch->regs[0x05] = (y >> 8) & 0x0f;
+    g_ft_touch->regs[0x06] = y & 0xff;
+    /*
+     * The touch controller owns INT.  Force the external line instead of
+     * only changing the pull source: Core2's RTC GPIO path leaves the GPIO
+     * output stage enabled even after pinMode(input_pullup), and an external
+     * low must still win over that high output latch on an open-drain line.
+     */
+    esp32_gpio_force_line(int_pin, !pressed);
+    if (getenv("M5SW_FT_TRACE")) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[ft5x06-input] pressed=%d x=%d y=%d int_pin=%d\n",
+                pressed, x, y, int_pin);
+    }
+}
+
+void m5sw_keyboard_event(uint8_t event)
+{
+    if (g_key_event_count == ARRAY_SIZE(g_key_events)) {
+        return;
+    }
+    g_key_events[(g_key_event_head + g_key_event_count) % ARRAY_SIZE(g_key_events)] = event;
+    g_key_event_count++;
+    esp32_gpio_set_input(11, 0);
+}
+
+void m5sw_plc_key_set(int key, bool pressed)
+{
+    if (key < 0 || key > 2) {
+        return;
+    }
+    uint8_t bit = 1u << (2 - key);
+    if (pressed) {
+        g_plc_keys &= ~bit;
+    } else {
+        g_plc_keys |= bit;
+    }
+}
+
 static inline uint8_t bcd(int v) { return ((v / 10) << 4) | (v % 10); }
 static inline int unbcd(uint8_t v) { return (v >> 4) * 10 + (v & 0xf); }
 
+static uint8_t sht4x_crc8(const uint8_t *data, int size)
+{
+    uint8_t crc = 0xff;
+    for (int i = 0; i < size; i++) {
+        crc ^= data[i];
+        for (int bit = 0; bit < 8; bit++) {
+            crc = (crc & 0x80) ? (uint8_t)((crc << 1) ^ 0x31)
+                               : (uint8_t)(crc << 1);
+        }
+    }
+    return crc;
+}
+
+static uint16_t encode_be16(double value, double resolution)
+{
+    int32_t raw = (int32_t)lrint(value / resolution);
+    raw = MAX(-32768, MIN(raw, 65535));
+    return (uint16_t)raw;
+}
+
+static uint16_t axp192_adc12(double value)
+{
+    int64_t raw = (int64_t)lrint(value);
+    return (uint16_t)MAX(0, MIN(raw, 0x0fff));
+}
+
+static uint16_t axp192_adc13(double value)
+{
+    int64_t raw = (int64_t)lrint(value);
+    return (uint16_t)MAX(0, MIN(raw, 0x1fff));
+}
+
+static uint16_t ina226_u16(M5swI2CDev *s, double value)
+{
+    int64_t raw = (int64_t)lrint(value);
+    return (uint16_t)MAX(0, MIN(raw, 65535));
+}
+
+static uint16_t ina3221_u16(double value)
+{
+    int64_t raw = (int64_t)lrint(value);
+    return (uint16_t)MAX(0, MIN(raw, 65535));
+}
+
+static uint16_t ina3221_reg16(M5swI2CDev *s, uint8_t reg)
+{
+    double shunt_mv;
+
+    switch (reg) {
+    case 0x00:
+        return 0x7127;                         /* continuous all channels */
+    case 0x01:
+    case 0x03:
+    case 0x05: {
+        uint32_t channel = (reg - 0x01) / 2;
+        shunt_mv = s->ina3221_current_ma[channel]
+                   * s->ina3221_shunt_ohms;
+        return ina3221_u16(shunt_mv / 0.04);   /* 40 uV/LSB */
+    }
+    case 0x02:
+    case 0x04:
+    case 0x06: {
+        uint32_t channel = (reg - 0x02) / 2;
+        return ina3221_u16(s->ina3221_bus_mv[channel] / 8.0);
+    }
+    case 0xFE:
+        return 0x5449;                         /* TI manufacturer ID */
+    case 0xFF:
+        return 0x3220;                         /* INA3221 die ID */
+    default:
+        return 0;
+    }
+}
+
+static uint16_t ina226_current_raw(M5swI2CDev *s)
+{
+    uint16_t cal = ((uint16_t)s->regs[0x05] << 8) | s->regs[0x06];
+    if (!cal || s->shunt_ohms <= 0.0) {
+        return 0;
+    }
+    double current_lsb = 0.00512 / ((double)cal * s->shunt_ohms);
+    return ina226_u16(s, (s->current_ma / 1000.0) / current_lsb);
+}
+
+static uint16_t bmm150_raw(double value, bool z_axis)
+{
+    static const double resolution = 10.0 * 4912.0 / 32760.0;
+    int32_t raw = (int32_t)lrint(value / resolution);
+    raw = MAX(-8192, MIN(raw, 8191));
+    return z_axis ? (uint16_t)((raw << 1) & ~1) : (uint16_t)(raw << 2);
+}

 {
     time_t t = time(NULL) + s->rtc_offset;
@@ -88,16 +414,160 @@ static void rx8130_time_written(M5swI2CDev *s)
     s->rtc_offset = (int64_t)t - (int64_t)time(NULL);
 }
 
+static void pcf8563_fill_time(M5swI2CDev *s)
+{
+    time_t t = time(NULL) + s->rtc_offset;
+    struct tm tm;
+    gmtime_r(&t, &tm);
+    s->regs[0x02] = bcd(tm.tm_sec);
+    s->regs[0x03] = bcd(tm.tm_min);
+    s->regs[0x04] = bcd(tm.tm_hour);
+    s->regs[0x05] = bcd(tm.tm_mday);
+    s->regs[0x06] = tm.tm_wday;
+    s->regs[0x07] = bcd(tm.tm_mon + 1);
+    s->regs[0x08] = bcd(tm.tm_year % 100);
+}
+
+static void pcf8563_time_written(M5swI2CDev *s)
+{
+    struct tm tm = {0};
+    tm.tm_sec = unbcd(s->regs[0x02] & 0x7f);
+    tm.tm_min = unbcd(s->regs[0x03] & 0x7f);
+    tm.tm_hour = unbcd(s->regs[0x04] & 0x3f);
+    tm.tm_mday = unbcd(s->regs[0x05] & 0x3f);
+    tm.tm_mon = unbcd(s->regs[0x07] & 0x1f) - 1;
+    tm.tm_year = unbcd(s->regs[0x08]) + 100;
+    time_t t = timegm(&tm);
+    s->rtc_offset = (int64_t)t - (int64_t)time(NULL);
+}
+
+static void st25_set_fifo(M5swI2CDev *s, const uint8_t *data, uint32_t size)
+{
+    size = MIN(size, sizeof(s->st25_fifo));
+    if (size) {
+        memcpy(s->st25_fifo, data, size);
+    }
+    s->st25_fifo_len = size;
+    s->st25_fifo_pos = 0;
+}
+
+static void st25_set_fifo_byte(M5swI2CDev *s, uint8_t value)
+{
+    st25_set_fifo(s, &value, 1);
+}
+
+static void st25_init_tag(M5swI2CDev *s)
+{
+    static const uint8_t uid[4] = { 0xde, 0xad, 0xbe, 0xef };
+    static const uint8_t page4[4] = { 'M', '5', 'N', '1' };
+
+    memset(s->st25_tag, 0, sizeof(s->st25_tag));
+    memcpy(s->st25_tag, uid, sizeof(uid));
+    s->st25_tag[4] = uid[0] ^ uid[1] ^ uid[2] ^ uid[3];
+    s->st25_tag[5] = 0x48;
+    s->st25_tag[6] = 0x00;
+    s->st25_tag[7] = 0x00;
+    s->st25_tag[12] = 0xe1;  /* NDEF capability container */
+    s->st25_tag[13] = 0x10;
+    s->st25_tag[14] = 0x12;  /* NTAG213 user memory */
+    s->st25_tag[15] = 0x00;
+    memcpy(&s->st25_tag[16], page4, sizeof(page4));
+}
+
+static void st25_execute_command(M5swI2CDev *s, uint8_t command)
+{
+    static const uint8_t atqa[2] = { 0x44, 0x00 };
+    static const uint8_t version[10] = {
+        0x00, 0x04, 0x04, 0x02, 0x02, 0x01, 0x0b, 0x03, 0x00, 0x00,
+    };
+    uint8_t response[18] = { 0 };
+
+    switch (command) {
+    case 0xc1: /* SET_DEFAULT */
+        memset(s->regs, 0, sizeof(s->regs));
+        memset(s->st25_space_b, 0, sizeof(s->st25_space_b));
+        s->regs[0x3f] = 0x2a;
+        st25_set_fifo(s, NULL, 0);
+        break;
+    case 0xc6: /* TRANSMIT_REQA */
+    case 0xc7: /* TRANSMIT_WUPA */
+        st25_set_fifo(s, atqa, sizeof(atqa));
+        break;
+    case 0xc5: /* TRANSMIT_WITHOUT_CRC */
+        if (s->st25_tx_len >= 1 && s->st25_tx[0] == 0x93) {
+            response[0] = s->st25_tag[0];
+            response[1] = s->st25_tag[1];
+            response[2] = s->st25_tag[2];
+            response[3] = s->st25_tag[3];
+            response[4] = s->st25_tag[4];
+            st25_set_fifo(s, response, 5);
+        }
+        break;
+    case 0xc4: /* TRANSMIT_WITH_CRC */
+        if (s->st25_tx_len >= 2 && s->st25_tx[0] == 0x93) {
+            response[0] = 0x00; /* SAK: MIFARE Ultralight / NTAG */
+            st25_set_fifo(s, response, 1);
+        } else if (s->st25_tx_len >= 1 && s->st25_tx[0] == 0x60) {
+            st25_set_fifo(s, version, sizeof(version));
+        } else if (s->st25_tx_len >= 2 && s->st25_tx[0] == 0x30) {
+            uint32_t base = (s->st25_tx[1] & ~3u) * 4;
+            uint32_t available = sizeof(s->st25_tag) - MIN(base,
+                                                         sizeof(s->st25_tag));
+            uint32_t copy = MIN(available, 16);
+            memcpy(response, &s->st25_tag[base], copy);
+            st25_set_fifo(s, response, copy + 2);
+        } else if (s->st25_tx_len >= 6 && s->st25_tx[0] == 0xa2) {
+            uint32_t page = s->st25_tx[1] & 0x3f;
+            uint32_t offset = page * 4;
+            if (offset + 4 <= sizeof(s->st25_tag)) {
+                memcpy(&s->st25_tag[offset], &s->st25_tx[2], 4);
+            }
+            st25_set_fifo_byte(s, 0x0a);
+        } else if (s->st25_tx_len >= 1 && s->st25_tx[0] == 0x50) {
+            st25_set_fifo(s, NULL, 0);
+        }
+        break;
+    case 0xdb: /* CLEAR_FIFO */
+        st25_set_fifo(s, NULL, 0);
+        s->st25_tx_len = 0;
+        break;
+    default:
+        break;
+    }
+    s->regs[0x1a] = command == 0xc8 ? 0x80 : s->regs[0x1a];
+}

 static int dev_send(I2CSlave *i2c, uint8_t data)
 {
     M5swI2CDev *s = M5SW_I2C_DEV(i2c);
+    if (s->model == M_ST25R3916) {
+        if (s->trace) {
+            qemu_log("[st25r3916] tx mode=%u data=%02x\n",
+                     s->st25_mode, data);
+        }
+        switch (s->st25_mode) {
+        case 0:
+            if (data == 0xfb) {
+                s->st25_mode = 2;
+            } else if (data == 0x80) {
+                s->st25_tx_len = 0;
+                s->st25_mode = 4;
+            } else if (data >= 0xc0) {
+                st25_execute_command(s, data);
+                s->st25_mode = 5;
+            } else {
+                s->ptr = data;
+                s->ptr_set = true;
+                s->st25_mode = 1;
+            }
+            return 0;
+        case 1:
+            s->regs[s->ptr] = data;
+            s->ptr++;
+            return 0;
+        case 2:
+            s->ptr = data & 0x3f;
+            s->ptr_set = true;
+            s->st25_mode = 3;
+            return 0;
+        case 3:
+            s->st25_space_b[s->ptr & 0x3f] = data;
+            s->st25_mode = 0;
+            return 0;
+        case 4:
+            if (s->st25_tx_len < sizeof(s->st25_tx)) {
+                s->st25_tx[s->st25_tx_len++] = data;
+            }
+            return 0;
+        default:
+            return 0;
+        }
+    }
+    if (s->model == M_SHT40 && !s->ptr_set) {
+        s->command = data;
+        s->ptr = 0;
+        s->ptr_set = true;
+        return 0;
+    }

 static void esp32_spi_do_command(Esp32SpiState* state, uint32_t cmd_reg);
 
+static void esp32_spi_m5_dma_panel(Esp32SpiState *s, uint32_t link)
+{
+    uint32_t desc_addr = 0x3ff00000u | (link & 0xfffff);
+
+    while (true) {
+        uint32_t desc[3];
+        uint32_t length, eof, buf;
+
+        cpu_physical_memory_read(desc_addr, desc, sizeof(desc));
+        length = (desc[0] >> 12) & 0xfff;
+        eof = (desc[0] >> 30) & 1;
+        buf = desc[1];
+        if (length) {
+            uint8_t *data = g_malloc(length);
+            cpu_physical_memory_read(buf, data, length);
+            m5_spi_panel_write((M5SpiPanelState *)s->m5_panel,
+                               data, length, false);
+            g_free(data);
+        }
+        if (eof || desc[2] == 0) {
+            break;
+        }
+        desc_addr = desc[2];
+    }
+}
+

+/* M5Stack Core2 (ESP32-D0WDQ6-V3 + ILI9342 320x240 + AXP192/FT5x06) */
+static void m5core2_machine_init(MachineState *machine)

+static void m5core2_machine_class_init(ObjectClass *oc, void *data)
+{
+    MachineClass *mc = MACHINE_CLASS(oc);

diff --git a/hw/xtensa/esp32s3_intc.c b/hw/xtensa/esp32s3_intc.c

--- a/hw/xtensa/esp32s3_intc.c

@@ -26,21 +26,73 @@
 
 #define IRQ_MAP(cpu, input) s->irq_map[cpu][input]
 
+static int esp32s3_intmatrix_output_index(Esp32s3IntMatrixState *s,
+                                          int cpu_index, int out_index)
+{
+    for (int int_index = 0;
+         int_index < s->cpu[cpu_index]->env.config->nextint; ++int_index) {
+        if (s->cpu[cpu_index]->env.config->extint[int_index] == out_index) {
+            return int_index;
+        }
+    }
+    return -1;
+}
+
+static void esp32s3_intmatrix_update_line(Esp32s3IntMatrixState *s,
+                                          int cpu_index, int out_index)
+{
+    if (s->outputs[cpu_index] == NULL || out_index == INTMATRIX_UNINT_VALUE) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    int int_index = esp32s3_intmatrix_output_index(s, cpu_index, out_index);
+    if (int_index < 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    int level = 0;
+    for (int source = 0; source < ESP32S3_INT_MATRIX_INPUTS; ++source) {
+        if (IRQ_MAP(cpu_index, source) == out_index &&
+            s->input_levels[source]) {
+            level = 1;
+            break;
+        }
+    }
+    qemu_set_irq(s->outputs[cpu_index][int_index], level);
+}
+
+static void esp32s3_intmatrix_set_route(Esp32s3IntMatrixState *s,
+                                        int cpu_index, int source_index,
+                                        int out_index)
+{
+    int old_out = s->source_out[cpu_index][source_index];
+
+    IRQ_MAP(cpu_index, source_index) = out_index;
+    s->source_out[cpu_index][source_index] = out_index;
+    if (old_out != out_index) {
+        esp32s3_intmatrix_update_line(s, cpu_index, old_out);
+    }
+    esp32s3_intmatrix_update_line(s, cpu_index, out_index);
+}
+
 static void esp32s3_intmatrix_irq_handler(void *opaque, int n, int level)
 {
     Esp32s3IntMatrixState *s = ESP32S3_INTMATRIX(opaque);
-    for (int i = 0; i < ESP32S3_CPU_COUNT; ++i) {
-        if (s->outputs[i] == NULL) {
